Result&lt;Value, ClewError&gt; &#123;&#10;    match command &#123;&#10;        Command::Publish &#123;&#10;            root,&#10;            proposal,&#10;            unassessed: &#95;,&#10;        &#125; =&gt; &#123;&#10;            let repo = Repository::open(&amp;root)?;&#10;            let artifact = load(&amp;repo, &amp;proposal)?;&#10;            let work = work::load(&amp;repo, &amp;artifact.work)?;&#10;            result&#91;&quot;proposal&quot;&#93; = json!(proposal);&#10;            Ok(result)&#10;        &#125;&#10;        Command::Submit &#123; root, work, input &#125; =&gt; submit(&#10;            &amp;Repository::open(&amp;root)?,&#10;            &amp;work,&#10;            store::read(&amp;input, store::MAX&#95;RECORD)?,&#10;        ),&#10;        Command::Show &#123;&#10;            root,&#10;            proposal,&#10;            cursor,&#10;            limit,&#10;        &#125; =&gt; show(&#10;            &amp;Repository::open(&amp;root)?,&#10;            &amp;proposal,&#10;            cursor.as&#95;deref(),&#10;            limit as usize,&#10; Two&#10;/// independent scoped checks must never overwrite each other&#39;s newer siblings.&#10;pub(crate) fn run&#95;and&#95;save&#95;selected(&#10;    repository: &amp;Repository,&#10;    selected: &amp;BTreeSet&lt;String&gt;,&#10;    debug&#95;output: Option&lt;&amp;crate::maven&#95;diagnostics::DebugOutput&gt;,&#10;) -&gt; Result&lt;(Check, String), ClewError&gt; &#123;&#10;    let &#95;run&#95;lock = acquire&#95;repository&#95;run&#95;lock(repository)?;&#10;    let checked = run&#95;selected&#95;unlocked(repository, selected, debug&#95;output)?;&#10;    let snapshot = checked.save&#95;snapshot(repository)?;&#10;    Ok((checked, snapshot))&#10;&#125;</pre></section><section id="ref-790275094a8be4b6d4b6d75e39538d1d396babd9721fb55c20fedf5e24d6b9b3"><h2>crates/clew/src/documentation/work.rs:2060–2062</h2><p>Citation: citation-8090b9be5962c3307c8e8f76. For an oversized required SOURCE, use docs work read-part with its SOURCE reference; for required retained content, use docs work read-retained-part with its exact kind/id. Continue until nextCursor is null; automatic authoring still requires the complete context to fit in its actual request.&quot;&#125;));&#10;    &#125;&#10;    for obligation in work.obligations.iter().filter(|o| &#123;&#10;        matches!(&#10;            o&#91;&quot;kind&quot;&#93;.as&#95;str(),&#10;            Some(&quot;MISSING&#95;SERVICE&quot; | &quot;MISSING&#95;EXTERNAL&#95;INPUT&quot;)&#10;        )&#10;    &#125;) &#123;&#10;        diagnostics.push(json!(&#123;&quot;code&quot;:&quot;MISSING&#95;WORK&#95;EVIDENCE&quot;,&quot;obligation&quot;:obligation,&quot;nextAction&quot;:&quot;Restore or register the required input and prepare new work before author review.&quot;&#125;));&#10;    &#125;&#10;    if state.untracked&#95;reads &#123;&#10;        diagnostics.push(json!(&#123;&quot;code&quot;:&quot;INCOMPLETE&#95;INFLUENCE&quot;,&quot;nextAction&quot;:&quot;Prepare a new work with every required input registered and use recorded reads only.&quot;&#125;));&#10;    &#125;&#10;    let status = if !diagnostics.is&#95;empty() &#123;&#10;        &quot;NEEDS&#95;REPAIR&quot;&#10;    &#125; else if !input.uncertainties.is&#95;empty()&#10;        || !input.gaps.is&#95;empty()&#10;        || !work.obligations.is&#95;empty()&#10;        || input.operations.iter().any(|operation| &#123;&#10;            operation&#10;                .visuals&#10;                .iter()&#10;                .flatten()&#10;                .any(|visual| !visual.limitations.is&#95;empty())&#10;        &#125;)&#10;        || narrative.as&#95;ref().is&#95;some&#95;and(|narrative| &#123;&#10;            narrative.operations.iter().any(|operation| &#123;&#10;                input&#10;                    .retained&#95;edits&#10;                    .iter()&#10;                    .any(|edit| edit.id == operation.id)&#10;                    &amp;&amp; (!operation.boundaries.is&#95;empty()&#10;                        || operation&#10;                            .visuals&#10;                            .iter()&#10;                            .any(|visual| !visual.limitations.is&#95;empty()))&#10;            &#125;)&#10;        &#125;)&#10;        || claims.values().any(|c| !c&#91;&quot;uncertainty&quot;&#93;.is&#95;null())&#10;    &#123;&#10;        &quot;READY&#95;WITH&#95;LIMITATIONS&quot;&#10;    &#125; else &#123;&#10;        &quot;READY&#95;FOR&#95;REVIEW&quot;&#10;    &#125;;&#10;    let artifact = Artifact &#123;&#10;        schema: &quot;codeclew-documentation-proposal-result/1.0&quot;.into(),&#10;        id: String::new(),&#10;        work: id.into(),&#10;        input,&#10;        narrative,&#10;        status: status.into(),&#10;        diagnostics,&#10;        claims,&#10;        read&#95;digest,&#10;        influence: work.influence.clone(),&#10;        meaning&#95;review: &quot;UNASSESSED&quot;.into(),&#10;    &#125;;&#10;    let stored = StoredArtifact::from&#95;runtime(artifact)?;&#10;    let artifact = &amp;stored.artifact;&#10;    let encoded = bytes(&amp;stored)?;&#10;    if encoded.len() &gt; 4 &#42; 1024 &#42; 1024 &#123;&#10;        return Err(invalid(&quot;materialized proposal exceeds 4 MiB&quot;));&#10;    &#125;&#10;    &#123;&#10;        let &#95;lock = repo.lock()?;&#10;        if digest(&amp;work::read&#95;state(repo, id)?)? != artifact.read&#95;digest &#123;&#10;            return Err(invalid(&#10;                &quot;work reads changed during proposal submission; retry submission&quot;,&#10;            ));&#10;        &#125;&#10;        let path = file(&amp;artifact.id)?;&#10;        if repo.path(&amp;path)?.exists() &#123;&#10;            load(repo, &amp;artifact.id)?;&#10;        &#125; else &#123;&#10;            repo.atomic(&amp;path, &amp;encoded)?;&#10;        &#125;&#10;    &#125;&#10;    show(repo, &amp;artifact.id, None, 20)&#10;&#125;</pre></section><section id="ref-f47ba024e022bbf68a393ce2e8c73b8697427f56153c2570857ae7522ff029ac"><h2>crates/clew/src/documentation/check.rs:189–189</h2><p>Citation: citation-bb422bafc4f7939687f565c4.    &#125; else &#123;&#10;        &quot;READY&#95;FOR&#95;REVIEW&quot;&#10;    &#125;;&#10;    let artifact = Artifact &#123;&#10;        schema: &quot;codeclew-documentation-proposal-result/1.0&quot;.into(),&#10;        id: String::new(),&#10;        work: id.into(),&#10;        input,&#10;        narrative,&#10;        status: status.into(),&#10;        diagnostics,&#10;        claims,&#10;        read&#95;digest,&#10;        influence: work.influence.clone(),&#10;        meaning&#95;review: &quot;UNASSESSED&quot;.into(),&#10;    &#125;;&#10;    let stored = StoredArtifact::from&#95;runtime(artifact)?;&#10;    let artifact = &amp;stored.artifact;&#10;    let encoded = bytes(&amp;stored)?;&#10;    if encoded.len() &gt; 4 &#42; 1024 &#42; 1024 &#123;&#10;        return Err(invalid(&quot;materialized proposal exceeds 4 MiB&quot;));&#10;    &#125;&#10;    validate&#95;context&#95;profile(&amp;subject, &amp;request)?;&#10;    validate&#95;http&#95;api&#95;contract&#95;profile(&amp;subject, &amp;request, &amp;checked)?;&#10;    validate&#95;endpoint&#95;context&#95;profile(&amp;subject, &amp;request, &amp;checked)?;&#10;    validate&#95;process&#95;graph&#95;root(&amp;subject, &amp;request, &amp;checked)?;&#10;    super::source&#95;data&#95;context::validate&#95;request(&amp;request)?;&#10;    normalize&#95;operation&#95;authoring&#95;contract(&amp;mut request)?;&#10;    let maintained&#95;context =&#10;        super::maintained&#95;context::load(repo, &amp;subject, &amp;mut request, &amp;checked)?;&#10;    let baseline = bindings::baseline(repo)?;&#10;    if request.documentation&#95;language.is&#95;none() &#123;&#10; == subject)&#10;        .cloned()&#10;        .collect();&#10;    review&#95;reasons.extend(&#10;        changes&#91;&quot;catalogueChanges&quot;&#93;&#10;            .as&#95;array()&#10;            .into&#95;iter()&#10; &#123;&#10;        for (i, note) in super::notes::for&#95;service(&amp;checked, id).enumerate() &#123;&#10;            handles.insert(&#10;                format!(&quot;note&#123;&#125;&quot;, i + 1),&#10;            .get(id)&#10;            .is&#95;some&#95;and(|scenario| scenario.process.is&#95;some())&#10;    &#123;&#10;        for (index, root) in super::processes::expected(&amp;checked, id)&#10;            .into&#95;iter()&#10;            .filter(|root| root == id || root == super::processes::OVERVIEW)&#10;            .enumerate()&#10;        &#123;&#10;            handles.insert(&#10;                format!(&quot;process&#123;&#125;&quot;, index + 1),&#10;                Handle &#123;&#10;                    kind: &quot;PROCESS&#95;ROOT&quot;.into(),&#10;                    id: root,&#10;                &#125;,&#10;            );&#10;        &#125;&#10;    &#125;&#10;    let mut influence: BTreeMap&lt;String, String&gt; = checked&#10;        .dependencies&#10;        .iter()&#10;        .filter(|(id, o)| &#123;&#10;            !(o.kind.starts&#95;with(&quot;PROCESS&#95;&quot;) || o.kind.starts&#95;with(&quot;VIEW&#95;&quot;))&#10;    let mut obligations = Vec::new();&#10;    for (service, failure) in &amp;checked.unresolved &#123;&#10;        if failure&#91;&quot;reason&quot;&#93; != &quot;SERVICE&#95;NOT&#95;SELECTED&quot; &#123;&#10;            obligations.push(json!(&#123;&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;MISSING&#95;SERVICE&quot;,&quot;service&quot;:service,&quot;detail&quot;:failure&#125;));&#10;        &#125;&#10;    &#125;&#10;    for (service, evidence) in &amp;checked.services &#123;&#10;        for boundary in &amp;evidence.boundaries &#123;&#10;            obligations&#10;                .push(json!(&#123;&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;EVIDENCE&#95;BOUNDARY&quot;,&quot;service&quot;:service,&quot;detail&quot;:boundary&#125;));&#10;        &#125;&#10;        if evidence.extractor == SOURCE&#95;EXTRACTOR &#123;&#10;            obligations.push(json!(&#123;&quot;kind&quot;:&quot;UNRESOLVED&#95;CALL&#95;AUTHORITY&quot;,&quot;service&quot;:service,&quot;detail&quot;:&quot;Syntax evidence does not establish runtime dispatch or configuration.        &#125;&#10;    &#125;&#10;    let mut work = Work &#123;&#10;        schema: &quot;codeclew-documentation-work/1.0&quot;.into(),&#10;        id: String::new(),&#10;        subject,&#10;        request,&#10;
